EHI Export Technical Details

Edit in Google Drive

Introduction

The Electronic Health Information (EHI) Export provides authorized users with the ability to download the designated record set (DRS) as defined by 45 CFR 164.501 in a computable format for one or multiple patients as defined by §170.315(b)(10). The export from WebChart provides DRS data in JSON format as described below.

For details on the format of the JSON standard, please see the JSON Data Interchange Syntax.

System Requirements

An EHI Export for a provider or an entire system can be an intensive process, especially when there is a large volume of patients or many years of history. To support this process, the system performing the export should maintain the following minimum requirements:

  • 8GB RAM
  • Available hard drive space of at least 100MB/patient being exported

In the event a customer does not have the hardware to perform such a process, assistance from MIE can be requested.

Designated Record Set Content

Object Description
accommodation_types Short codes for accommodations paired with a longer description
accommodations Modifications that allow an employee to continue working while not permitted to perform some aspect of their job
appointments Patient appointment details
apt_types Coded reasons for a patient appointment
audio Discrete data for occupational audiogram tests
audio_baseline Baseline audiogram tests for comparison with future results
body_part_types Descriptions for location of occupational injury
clinical_restriction_types Short codes for clinical restrictions paired with a longer description
coding_link Generic linking information between patient chart content and a code system/code pair
conversation_messages Messages between a patient or representative and their care team
dicom_images DICOM image files provided in Base64
dicom_series Information about a DICOM series under a study
dicom_studies Information about a DICOM study
dictation Dictation voice files
dictation_documents Pairing transcribed documents to the original audio file
dictation_jobtypes Types of dictation jobs
document_attachments Files associated with a document
document_sign Document e-sign requests
document_types Short codes for a type of document linked with an associated LOINC code and long description
documents Documents for a patient with content provided in Base64
documents_link Associates between multiple documents
documents_txt Plain text content of a document or identifier for a file location
encounter_dictation Voice files for sections of an encounter
encounter_documents Documents generated for or by an encounter
encounter_extended_index Descriptions for encounter_extended_values
encounter_extended_values Additional information for encounters not traditionally captured
encounter_order_status Status descriptions for encounter_orders
encounter_orders Orders generated for or by an encounter
encounter_visit_types Short codes for types of encounters paired with a description
encounters Data related to a patient visit
encounters_link Links of additional data (conditions, observations, etc) to an encounter
escript_cancelrx
escript_changereq
escript_error
escript_message
escript_newrx
escript_refreq
escript_refres_sign
escript_rxfill
extended_value_names Descriptions for additional task information
insurance_policy Information regarding a patient's insurance policy
insurance_pre_cert Prior authorization for encounter orders
incident_bls
incident_condition_link
incident_extended_fields Additional information for incidents usually related to workers compensation interfaces
incident_links Links to additional data regarding an incident
incident_nibp Details regarding the nature of injury and body part for an incident
incident_users
incidents Report of a workplace injury for an employee
injections Vaccinations and injectable medications
insurance_plans Information for insurance companies
lab_requests Lab result observations grouped to be displayed in a document
lab_requests_txt Plain text content of a lab request or identifier for a file location
locations Systems and physical locations
multi_referrer_apt Users that referred a patient for an appointment
multi_resource_apt Users that are a resource for an appointment
multi_type_apt All appointment types for an appointment
observations Discrete repeatable information regarding a patient (lab results, vitals, measurements, etc)
nature_of_injury_types
observation_codes A record for types of observations including a short code, description, and standardized units, ranges, or LOINC codes
observation_flags
observation_keys
observations_snomed SNOMED codes related to an encounter
order_items Orders for an order requisition
order_list A list or catalog of orderable items
order_question_answers Answers to questions related to order items
order_questions Configuration questions to answer for orders
order_request_icd9cm Conditions linked to order requisitions
order_requests Order requisitions stored in a document
panel Group of actions regularly scheduled to perform health surveillance
panel_decert Records of employees medically decertified for a job duty
panel_membership Employees participating in a panel
pat_pat_relflat Relationships between charts
pat_pat_relations Relationships between charts
patient_additional_info
patient_clinical_restriction Activities a patient is not permitted to perform
patient_conditions Conditions, diagnoses, problems for a patient
patient_conditions_family Conditions, diagnoses, problems present in a patient's family
patient_extended_index Descriptions of additional patient data
patient_extended_values Additional patient data
patient_mrns Identifiers for a patient
patient_panel_status Certification status for a patient in a panel
patient_partitions Groups available for organizing patients
patient_procedures Procedures for a patient
patient_respiratordetails Respirator mask details for approved employees
patient_targets
patients Details for a patient and the starting point for all DRS requests
pft Details of a pulmonary function test
pft_maneuver Individual efforts for each portion of a pft
relation_types
rxlist Current and historical medications for a patient
rxlist_allergylist Allergies for a patient
rxlist_refills Initial fill and refills for a patient's medication
rxlist_sign
storage_types Document storage formats
tasklist_extended_values Additional information for a task
tasks Documentation of user to user requests and records of actions taken
user_patients Relationship between a user and a patient
users Details for users of a system

Multiple Patients

When performing a multi-patient export, one JSON file will be generated for each exported patient. These files will then be compressed in a zip file.
